Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The models variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 263 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 949 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the models space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 263 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 949. QED.
